double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt =       \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n   // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n              unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n//        const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(),   // size nx*ny+1\n    std::vector<int> cell_points;  // point indices, ascending within each cell\n\n    inline int cellX(double x) const {\n        int c = static_cast<int>(x / cell);\n        return std::clamp(c, 0, nx - 1);\n    }\n    inline int cellY(double y) const {\n        int c = static_cast<int>(y / cell);\n        return std::clamp(c, 0, ny - 1);\n    }\n\n    Grid(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold) {\n        const int MAX_CELLS_PER_DIM = 1024;\n        cell = std::max(threshold, std::max(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT) / MAX_CELLS_PER_DIM);\n        nx = std::max(1, std::min(MAX_CELLS_PER_DIM, static_cast<int>(std::ceil(MAX_WIDTH / cell))));\n        ny = std::max(1, std::min(MAX_CELLS_PER_DIM, static_cast<int>(std::ceil(MAX_HEIGHT / cell))));\n        const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n        cell_start.assign(static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny + 1, 0);\n        std::vector<int> cid(N);\n        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n            cid[i] = cellY(points[i].y) * nx + cellX(points[i].x);\n            cell_start[cid[i] + 1]++;\n        }\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny; ++c) cell_start[c + 1] += cell_start[c];\n        cell_points.resize(N);\n        std::vector<int> fill(cell_start.begin(), cell_start.end() - 1);\n        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) cell_points[fill[cid[i]]++] = i;\n    }\n\n    // Call f(j) for every point j in the 3x3 cell block around point p\n    template <typename F>\n    inline void forNeighborhood(const Point& p, F&& f) const {\n        const int cx = cellX(p.x), cy = cellY(p.y);\n        const int x0 = std::max(0, cx - 1), x1 = std::min(nx - 1, cx + 1);\n        const int y0 = std::max(0, cy - 1), y1 = std::min(ny - 1, cy + 1);\n        for (int yy = y0; yy <= y1; ++yy) {\n            const int rs = cell_start[yy * nx + x0];\n            const int re = cell_start[yy * nx + x1 + 1];\n            for (int k = rs; k < re; ++k) f(cell_points[k]);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// Per-thread scratch buffers for candidate cluster generation\nstruct Workspace {\n    std::vector<int> idx;\n    std::vector<double> px, py, maxd;\n};\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\n//\n// Equivalent to the greedy procedure: repeatedly add the unclustered point\n// with the smallest maximum distance to all current members (ties broken by\n// lowest index) as long as that distance is below the threshold. The\n// per-candidate maximum distance is maintained incrementally, and candidates\n// whose maximum distance reached the threshold are discarded permanently\n// (the maximum can only grow).\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                             const std::vector<char>& clustered,\n                             const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                             const Grid& grid,\n                             const double threshold,\n                             Workspace& ws,\n                             std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<int>& idx = ws.idx;\n    idx.clear();\n    const Point& sp = points[seed_point];\n    grid.forNeighborhood(sp, [&](int j) {\n        if (j == seed_point || clustered[j]) return;\n        if (distance(points[j], sp) < threshold) idx.push_back(j);\n    });\n    std::sort(idx.begin(), idx.end());\n\n    int n = static_cast<int>(idx.size());\n    ws.px.resize(n);\n    ws.py.resize(n);\n    ws.maxd.resize(n);\n    double* __restrict px = ws.px.data();\n    double* __restrict py = ws.py.data();\n    double* __restrict md = ws.maxd.data();\n    int* __restrict ix = idx.data();\n\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        cluster_members->clear();\n        cluster_members->push_back(seed_point);\n    }\n\n    // Initial maximum distance to the cluster {seed}, and first selection\n    int best = -1;\n    double best_d = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    for (int k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n        const Point& c = points[ix[k]];\n        px[k] = c.x;\n        py[k] = c.y;\n        const double d = std::max(0.0, distance(c, sp));\n        md[k] = d;\n        if (d < best_d) { best_d = d; best = k; }\n    }\n\n    int size = 1;\n    while (best >= 0) {\n        // Add the selected candidate to the cluster\n        const Point np = {px[best], py[best]};\n        if (cluster_members) cluster_members->push_back(ix[best]);\n        ++size;\n\n        // Update maxima, drop the new member and invalid candidates,\n        // and find the next selection in a single pass (order preserved).\n        int w = 0;\n        int nb = -1;\n        double nbd = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n        for (int k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n            if (k == best) continue;\n            const Point c = {px[k], py[k]};\n            const double d = std::max(md[k], distance(c, np));\n            if (d >= threshold) continue;\n            ix[w] = ix[k];\n            px[w] = c.x;\n            py[w] = c.y;\n            md[w] = d;\n            if (d < nbd) { nbd = d; nb = w; }\n            ++w;\n        }\n        n = w;\n        best = nb;\n    }\n    return size;\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\n//\n// Candidate cluster cardinalities are cached per seed: a seed's candidate\n// cluster depends only on the clustered status of points within the threshold\n// distance of the seed, so it only needs recomputation when such a point gets\n// clustered. Dirty seeds are evaluated in parallel.\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices(N);\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    const Grid grid(points, threshold);\n\n    std::vector<int> cardinality(N, 0);\n    std::vector<char> dirty(N, 1);\n    std::vector<int> dirty_list;\n    dirty_list.reserve(N);\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) unclustered_indices[i] = i;\n\n    const int nthreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    std::vector<Workspace> workspaces(nthreads);\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        dirty_list.clear();\n        for (int s : unclustered_indices) {\n            if (dirty[s]) { dirty_list.push_back(s); dirty[s] = 0; }\n        }\n        const int ndirty = static_cast<int>(dirty_list.size());\n        const int nunc = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n\n        #pragma omp parallel\n        {\n            Workspace& ws = workspaces[omp_get_thread_num()];\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)\n            for (int i = 0; i < ndirty; ++i) {\n                const int seed = dirty_list[i];\n                cardinality[seed] = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, grid,\n                                                             threshold, ws);\n            }\n\n            // Argmax over seeds: largest cardinality, lowest seed index on ties\n            int lc = -1, ls = -1;\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n            for (int i = 0; i < nunc; ++i) {\n                const int s = unclustered_indices[i];\n                if (cardinality[s] > lc) { lc = cardinality[s]; ls = s; }\n            }\n            #pragma omp critical\n            {\n                if (lc > max_cardinality || (lc == max_cardinality && ls >= 0 && ls < best_seed)) {\n                    max_cardinality = lc;\n                    best_seed = ls;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            generateCandidateCluster(best_seed, clustered, points, grid, threshold,\n                                     workspaces[0], &cluster.members);\n\n            // Mark all members as clustered and invalidate affected seeds\n            for (const int m : cluster.members) {\n                clustered[m] = 1;\n            }\n            for (const int m : cluster.members) {\n                const Point& mp = points[m];\n                grid.forNeighborhood(mp, [&](int j) {\n                    if (!clustered[j]) dirty[j] = 1;\n                });\n            }\n            clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new+src[end:]\nsrc=src.replace('#include <vector>\\n','#include <vector>\\n\\n#include <omp.h>\\n',1)\nsrc=src.replace('Simplified Sequential Version','OpenMP Parallel Version',1)\nopen('qtclustering.cpp','w').write(src)\nEOF\ncat > CMakeLists.txt <<'EOF'\ncmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)\n\nproject(qtclustering)\n\nset(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)\nset(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)\n\nfind_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)\n\nadd_executable(qtclustering qtclustering.cpp)\n\ntarget_compile_options(qtclustering PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)\n\n# Link OpenMP and math library\ntarget_link_libraries(qtclustering PRIVATE OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX m)\nEOF\nmkdir -p /tmp/qtb && cd /tmp/qtb && cmake -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release /home/llmtest/evals/20261002-142720/qtclustering_claude-opus-5.5-cc-medium_omp_r1/qtclustering >/dev/null && make 2>&1 | grep -E \"warning|error|Built\""},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":456,"cache_read_input_tokens":19607,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":456},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"7c45b09d-ed76-40ca-9834-77f846198d1e","uuid":"e0d6d59b-c7c7-4845-8dd2-3c6608944cc8","timestamp":"2026-10-02T17:39:55.906Z","request_id":"req_011CfdpUgtbGmBpBT9ABkLqa"}

yy <= y1; ++yy) {\n            const int rs = cell_start[yy * nx + x0];\n            const int re = cell_start[yy * nx + x1 + 1];\n            for (int k = rs; k < re; ++k) f(cell_points[k]);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// Per-thread scratch buffers for candidate cluster generation\nstruct Workspace {\n    std::vector<int> idx;\n    std::vector<double> px, py, maxd;\n};\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\n//\n// Equivalent to the greedy procedure: repeatedly add the unclustered point\n// with the smallest maximum distance to all current members (ties broken by\n// lowest index) as long as that distance is below the threshold. The\n// per-candidate maximum distance is maintained incrementally, and candidates\n// whose maximum distance reached the threshold are discarded permanently\n// (the maximum can only grow).\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                             const std::vector<char>& clustered,\n                             const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                             const Grid& grid,\n                             const double threshold,\n                             Workspace& ws,\n                             std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<int>& idx = ws.idx;\n    idx.clear();\n    const Point& sp = points[seed_point];\n    grid.forNeighborhood(sp, [&](int j) {\n        if (j == seed_point || clustered[j]) return;\n        if (distance(points[j], sp) < threshold) idx.push_back(j);\n    });\n    std::sort(idx.begin(), idx.end());\n\n    int n = static_cast<int>(idx.size());\n    ws.px.resize(n);\n    ws.py.resize(n);\n    ws.maxd.resize(n);\n    double* __restrict px = ws.px.data();\n    double* __restrict py = ws.py.data();\n    double* __restrict md = ws.maxd.data();\n    int* __restrict ix = idx.data();\n\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        cluster_members->clear();\n        cluster_members->push_back(seed_point);\n    }\n\n    // Initial maximum distance to the cluster {seed}, and first selection\n    int best = -1;\n    double best_d = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    for (int k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n        const Point& c = points[ix[k]];\n        px[k] = c.x;\n        py[k] = c.y;\n        const double d = std::max(0.0, distance(c, sp));\n        md[k] = d;\n        if (d < best_d) { best_d = d; best = k; }\n    }\n\n    int size = 1;\n    while (best >= 0) {\n        // Add the selected candidate to the cluster\n        const Point np = {px[best], py[best]};\n        if (cluster_members) cluster_members->push_back(ix[best]);\n        ++size;\n\n        // Update maxima, drop the new member and invalid candidates,\n        // and find the next selection in a single pass (order preserved).\n        int w = 0;\n        int nb = -1;\n        double nbd = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n        for (int k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n            if (k == best) continue;\n            const Point c = {px[k], py[k]};\n            const double d = std::max(md[k], distance(c, np));\n            if (d >= threshold) continue;\n            ix[w] = ix[k];\n            px[w] = c.x;\n            py[w] = c.y;\n            md[w] = d;\n            if (d < nbd) { nbd = d; nb = w; }\n            ++w;\n        }\n        n = w;\n        best = nb;\n    }\n    return size;\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\n//\n// Candidate cluster cardinalities are cached per seed: a seed's candidate\n// cluster depends only on the clustered status of points within the threshold\n// distance of the seed, so it only needs recomputation when such a point gets\n// clustered. Dirty seeds are evaluated in parallel.\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices(N);\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    const Grid grid(points, threshold);\n\n    std::vector<int> cardinality(N, 0);\n    std::vector<char> dirty(N, 1);\n    std::vector<int> dirty_list;\n    dirty_list.reserve(N);\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) unclustered_indices[i] = i;\n\n    const int nthreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    std::vector<Workspace> workspaces(nthreads);\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        dirty_list.clear();\n        for (int s : unclustered_indices) {\n            if (dirty[s]) { dirty_list.push_back(s); dirty[s] = 0; }\n        }\n        const int ndirty = static_cast<int>(dirty_list.size());\n        const int nunc = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n\n        #pragma omp parallel\n        {\n            Workspace& ws = workspaces[omp_get_thread_num()];\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)\n            for (int i = 0; i < ndirty; ++i) {\n                const int seed = dirty_list[i];\n                cardinality[seed] = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, grid,\n                                                             threshold, ws);\n            }\n\n            // Argmax over seeds: largest cardinality, lowest seed index on ties\n            int lc = -1, ls = -1;\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n            for (int i = 0; i < nunc; ++i) {\n                const int s = unclustered_indices[i];\n                if (cardinality[s] > lc) { lc = cardinality[s]; ls = s; }\n            }\n            #pragma omp critical\n            {\n                if (lc > max_cardinality || (lc == max_cardinality && ls >= 0 && ls < best_seed)) {\n                    max_cardinality = lc;\n                    best_seed = ls;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            generateCandidateCluster(best_seed, clustered, points, grid, threshold,\n                                     workspaces[0], &cluster.members);\n\n            // Mark all members as clustered and invalidate affected seeds\n            for (const int m : cluster.members) {\n                clustered[m] = 1;\n            }\n            for (const int m : cluster.members) {\n                const Point& mp = points[m];\n                grid.forNeighborhood(mp, [&](int j) {\n                    if (!clustered[j]) dirty[j] = 1;\n                });\n            }\n            clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new+src[end:]\nsrc=src.replace('#include <vector>\\n','#include <vector>\\n\\n#include <omp.h>\\n',1)\nsrc=src.replace('Simplified Sequential Version','OpenMP Parallel Version',1)\nopen('qtclustering.cpp','w').write(src)\nEOF\ncat > CMakeLists.txt <<'EOF'\ncmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)\n\nproject(qtclustering)\n\nset(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)\nset(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)\n\nfind_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)\n\nadd_executable(qtclustering qtclustering.cpp)\n\ntarget_compile_options(qtclustering PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)\n\n# Link OpenMP and math library\ntarget_link_libraries(qtclustering PRIVATE OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX m)\nEOF\nmkdir -p /tmp/qtb && cd /tmp/qtb && cmake -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release /home/llmtest/evals/20261002-142720/qtclustering_claude-opus-5.5-cc-medium_omp_r1/qtclustering >/dev/null && make 2>&1 | grep -E \"warning|error|Built\"","is_backgrounded":false,"task_type":"local_bash","uuid":"a4d831da-e36b-4885-9040-568e17ad43e0","session_id":"7c45b09d-ed76-40ca-9834-77f846198d1e"}

Member lists of candidate\n275\t// clusters are cached as well (within a memory budget) so that the selected\n276\t// cluster does not have to be regenerated.\n277\tstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n278\t                                  const double threshold) {\n279\t    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n280\t    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n281\t    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices(N);\n282\t    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n283\t    const Grid grid(points, threshold);\n284\t\n285\t    std::vector<int> cardinality(N, 0);\n286\t    std::vector<char> dirty(N, 1);\n287\t    std::vector<std::pair<long long, int>> dirty_list;   // size nx*ny+1\n    std::vector<int> cell_points;  // point indices, ascending within each cell\n    std::vector<int> cell_of;      // cell id of each point\n\n    inline int cellX(double x) const {\n        int c = static_cast<int>(x / cell);\n        return std::clamp(c, 0, nx - 1);\n    }\n    inline int cellY(double y) const {\n        int c = static_cast<int>(y / cell);\n        return std::clamp(c, 0, ny - 1);\n    }\n\n    Grid(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold) {\n        const int MAX_CELLS_PER_DIM = 1024;\n        cell = std::max(threshold, std::max(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT) / MAX_CELLS_PER_DIM);\n        nx = std::max(1, std::min(MAX_CELLS_PER_DIM, static_cast<int>(std::ceil(MAX_WIDTH / cell))));\n        ny = std::max(1, std::min(MAX_CELLS_PER_DIM, static_cast<int>(std::ceil(MAX_HEIGHT / cell))));\n        const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n        cell_start.assign(static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny + 1, 0);\n        cell_of.resize(N);\n        std::vector<int>& cid = cell_of;\n        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n            cid[i] = cellY(points[i].y) * nx + cellX(points[i].x);\n            cell_start[cid[i] + 1]++;\n        }\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny; ++c) cell_start[c + 1] += cell_start[c];\n        cell_points.resize(N);\n        std::vector<int> fill(cell_start.begin(), cell_start.end() - 1);\n        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) cell_points[fill[cid[i]]++] = i;\n    }\n\n    // Call f(c) for every cell id c in the 3x3 cell block around point i\n    template <typename F>\n    inline void forBlockCells(const int i, F&& f) const {\n        const int cx = cell_of[i] % nx, cy = cell_of[i] / nx;\n        const int x0 = std::max(0, cx - 1), x1 = std::min(nx - 1, cx + 1);\n        const int y0 = std::max(0, cy - 1), y1 = std::min(ny - 1, cy + 1);\n        for (int yy = y0; yy <= y1; ++yy)\n            for (int xx = x0; xx <= x1; ++xx) f(yy * nx + xx);\n    }\n\n    // Call f(j) for every point j in the 3x3 cell block around point i\n    template <typename F>\n    inline void forNeighborhood(const int i, F&& f) const {\n        const int cx = cell_of[i] % nx, cy = cell_of[i] / nx;\n        const int x0 = std::max(0, cx - 1), x1 = std::min(nx - 1, cx + 1);\n        const int y0 = std::max(0, cy - 1), y1 = std::min(ny - 1, cy + 1);\n        for (int yy = y0; yy <= y1; ++yy) {\n            const int rs = cell_start[yy * nx + x0];\n            const int re = cell_start[yy * nx + x1 + 1];\n            for (int k = rs; k < re; ++k) f(cell_points[k]);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// Per-thread scratch buffers for candidate cluster generation\nstruct Workspace {\n    std::vector<int> idx;\n    std::vector<double> px, py, maxd;\n};\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\n//\n// Equivalent to the greedy procedure: repeatedly add the unclustered point\n// with the smallest maximum distance to all current members (ties broken by\n// lowest index) as long as that distance is below the threshold. The\n// per-candidate maximum distance is maintained incrementally, and candidates\n// whose maximum distance reached the threshold are discarded permanently\n// (the maximum can only grow).\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                             const std::vector<char>& clustered,\n                             const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                             const Grid& grid,\n                             const double threshold,\n                             Workspace& ws,\n                             std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<int>& idx = ws.idx;\n    idx.clear();\n    const Point& sp = points[seed_point];\n    grid.forNeighborhood(seed_point, [&](int j) {\n        if (j == seed_point || clustered[j]) return;\n        if (distance(points[j], sp) < threshold) idx.push_back(j);\n    });\n    std::sort(idx.begin(), idx.end());\n\n    int n = static_cast<int>(idx.size());\n    ws.px.resize(n);\n    ws.py.resize(n);\n    ws.maxd.resize(n);\n    double* __restrict px = ws.px.data();\n    double* __restrict py = ws.py.data();\n    double* __restrict md = ws.maxd.data();\n    int* __restrict ix = idx.data();\n\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        cluster_members->clear();\n        cluster_members->push_back(seed_point);\n    }\n\n    // Squared distances are tracked (max commutes with the monotonic sqrt).\n    // A candidate is valid while s < sq_limit; since the maximum only grows,\n    // invalid entries stay invalid and are removed by lazy compaction.\n    const double sq_limit = squaredLimit(threshold);\n    const double inf = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();\n\n    double mn = inf;\n    int live = 0;\n    for (int k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n        const Point& c = points[ix[k]];\n        px[k] = c.x;\n        py[k] = c.y;\n        const double v = sqDistance(c.x, c.y, sp.x, sp.y);\n        md[k] = v;\n        mn = (v < mn) ? v : mn;\n        live += (v < sq_limit);\n    }\n\n    int size = 1;\n    while (mn < sq_limit) {\n        // Lazily drop invalid candidates (order preserved)\n        if (2 * live < n) {\n            int w = 0;\n            for (int k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                const double v = md[k];\n                ix[w] = ix[k];\n                px[w] = px[k];\n                py[w] = py[k];\n                md[w] = v;\n                w += (v < sq_limit);\n            }\n            n = w;\n        }\n\n        // Select the lowest-index candidate whose distance sqrt(s) equals\n        // the minimum distance sqrt(mn), i.e. s in [mn, s_hi]\n        const double r = std::sqrt(mn);\n        double s_hi = mn;\n        for (;;) {\n            const double next = std::nextafter(s_hi, inf);\n            if (std::sqrt(next) != r) break;\n            s_hi = next;\n        }\n        int best = 0;\n        while (md[best] > s_hi) ++best;\n\n        // Add the selected candidate to the cluster\n        const double nx = px[best], ny = py[best];\n        if (cluster_members) cluster_members->push_back(ix[best]);\n        ++size;\n        md[best] = inf;\n\n        // Update maxima with the distance to the new member and find the\n        // minimum / number of valid candidates for the next selection\n        mn = inf;\n        live = 0;\n        #pragma omp simd reduction(min : mn) reduction(+ : live)\n        for (int k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n            const double m = md[k];\n            const double d = sqDistance(px[k], py[k], nx, ny);\n            const double v = (m < d) ? d : m;\n            md[k] = v;\n            mn = (v < mn) ? v : mn;\n            live += (v < sq_limit);\n        }\n    }\n    return size;\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\n//\n// Candidate cluster cardinalities are cached per seed: a seed's candidate\n// cluster depends only on the clustered status of points within the threshold\n// distance of the seed, so it only needs recomputation when such a point gets\n// clustered. Dirty seeds are evaluated in parallel. Member lists of candidate\n// clusters are cached as well (within a memory budget) so that the selected\n// cluster does not have to be regenerated.\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices(N);\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    const Grid grid(points, threshold);\n\n    std::vector<int> cardinality(N, 0);\n    std::vector<char> dirty(N, 1);\n    std::vector<std::pair<long long, int>> dirty_list;  // (estimated cost, seed)\n    dirty_list.reserve(N);\n\n    // Unclustered point count per grid cell (for work estimation)\n    std::vector<int> cell_count(grid.cell_start.size() - 1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < cell_count.size(); ++c)\n        cell_count[c] = grid.cell_start[c + 1] - grid.cell_start[c];\n\n    // Cached member lists, bounded by a total budget of stored indices\n    const long long MEMBER_CACHE_BUDGET = 1LL << 26;\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> cached_members(N);\n    std::vector<char> has_members(N, 0);\n    std::atomic<long long> cached_total{0};\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) unclustered_indices[i] = i;\n\n    const int nthreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    std::vector<Workspace> workspaces(nthreads);\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> thread_members(nthreads);\n\n    // Approximate cost units handled per thread before adding another thread\n    const long long WORK_PER_THREAD = 1LL << 16;\n    int team = -1;\n\n    std::vector<int> cell_stamp(cell_count.size(), -1);\n    std::vector<int> touched_cells;\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        dirty_list.clear();\n        long long total_work = 0;\n        for (int s : unclustered_indices) {\n            if (!dirty[s]) continue;\n            dirty[s] = 0;\n            long long nb = 0;\n            grid.forBlockCells(s, [&](int c) { nb += cell_count[c]; });\n            const long long w = nb * nb + 16;\n            total_work += w;\n            dirty_list.emplace_back(w, s);\n        }\n        // Most expensive seeds first for better dynamic load balance\n        std::sort(dirty_list.begin(), dirty_list.end(),\n                  [](const auto& a, const auto& b) {\n                      return a.first > b.first || (a.first == b.first && a.second < b.second);\n                  });\n        const int ndirty = static_cast<int>(dirty_list.size());\n        const int nunc = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n\n        if (ndirty > 0) {\n            // Fixed team size (chosen from the initial workload) avoids costly\n            // team resizing; iterations with little work run sequentially.\n            if (team < 0) {\n                team = static_cast<int>(std::clamp<long long>(\n                    total_work / WORK_PER_THREAD, 1, std::min(nthreads, ndirty)));\n            }\n            #pragma omp parallel num_threads(team) if (total_work >= WORK_PER_THREAD && ndirty > 1)\n            {\n                const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n                Workspace& ws = workspaces[tid];\n                std::vector<int>& members = thread_members[tid];\n                #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)\n                for (int i = 0; i < ndirty; ++i) {\n                    const int seed = dirty_list[i].second;\n                    const int card = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, grid,\n                                                              threshold, ws, &members);\n                    cardinality[seed] = card;\n                    // Cache the member list if within the (soft) memory budget\n                    std::vector<int>& cm = cached_members[seed];\n                    const long long old = has_members[seed] ? static_cast<long long>(cm.size()) : 0;\n                    if (cached_total.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) - old + card <= MEMBER_CACHE_BUDGET) {\n                        cm.assign(members.begin(), members.end());\n                        has_members[seed] = 1;\n                        cached_total.fetch_add(card - old, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n                    } else if (has_members[seed]) {\n                        std::vector<int>().swap(cm);\n                        has_members[seed] = 0;\n                        cached_total.fetch_sub(old, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Argmax over seeds: largest cardinality, lowest seed index on ties\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        #pragma omp parallel if (nunc >= 65536)\n        {\n            int lc = -1, ls = -1;\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n            for (int i = 0; i < nunc; ++i) {\n                const int s = unclustered_indices[i];\n                if (cardinality[s] > lc) { lc = cardinality[s]; ls = s; }\n            }\n            #pragma omp critical\n            {\n                if (lc > max_cardinality || (lc == max_cardinality && ls >= 0 && ls < best_seed)) {\n                    max_cardinality = lc;\n                    best_seed = ls;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            if (has_members[best_seed]) {\n                cluster.members = cached_members[best_seed];\n            } else {\n                generateCandidateCluster(best_seed, clustered, points, grid, threshold,\n                                         workspaces[0], &cluster.members);\n            }\n\n            // Mark all members as clustered and invalidate affected seeds\n            for (const int m : cluster.members) {\n                clustered[m] = 1;\n                cell_count[grid.cell_of[m]]--;\n                if (has_members[m]) {\n                    cached_total -= static_cast<long long>(cached_members[m].size());\n                    std::vector<int>().swap(cached_members[m]);\n                    has_members[m] = 0;\n                }\n            }\n            // (union of the 3x3 cell blocks around all members)\n            const int stamp = static_cast<int>(clusters.size());\n            touched_cells.clear();\n            for (const int m : cluster.members) {\n                grid.forBlockCells(m, [&](int c) {\n                    if (cell_stamp[c] != stamp) { cell_stamp[c] = stamp; touched_cells.push_back(c); }\n                });\n            }\n            for (const int c : touched_cells) {\n                for (int k = grid.cell_start[c]; k < grid.cell_start[c + 1]; ++k) {\n                    const int j = grid.cell_points[k];\n                    if (!clustered[j]) dirty[j] = 1;\n                }\n            }\n            clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {","numLines":370,"startLine":75,"totalLines":623}}}

On this 128-core EPYC (256 threads) it's about 50–70× faster than its own single-thread run. Against the original, 2000 points went from 32.6 s to well under a second.\n\n**Correctness checks:**\n- **Matches the original:** 9 configurations (N from 37 to 2000, thresholds from 0.05 to 25) produce identical `-r` membership output, cluster statistics and `-v` validation output. Only the timing lines differ.\n- **Same at any thread count:** for N up to 30000, results with 1 thread and with 256 threads are identical, and validation passes.\n- **Inputs I didn't compare:** larger N wasn't compared against the original, because it takes hours or more at those sizes.\n\n**Timings with this build** (clustering time):\n\n| Case | 1 thread | 64 | 128 | 256 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `-n 20000` | 12.3 s | 424 ms | 291 ms | 245 ms |\n| `-n 30000 -t 1.0` | 7.07 s | 337 ms | 255 ms | 295 ms |\n| `-n 10000 -t 5` | 18.8 s | 497 ms | 270 ms | 277 ms |\n\nThe machine is shared with other users. When it's busy, 256-thread runs can be several times slower, because one stalled thread holds up the others. One earlier run took 7 s instead of about 0.3 s.\n\n**What changed in `qtclustering.cpp`** (generator, validation, `main` and output are untouched):\n1. **Faster cluster building.** For each candidate point, the code now keeps a running \"farthest member\" distance instead of recomputing it against every member each step. Points that go over the threshold are dropped for good, since that distance can only grow. Selection and tie-breaking work exactly as before: the closest candidate wins and the lowest index breaks ties. This alone roughly halved single-thread time.\n2. **Neighbour grid.** A grid with cells at least as wide as the threshold means only the 3×3 block of cells around a seed is searched for candidates.\n3. **Reusing earlier results.** A seed's candidate cluster only depends on points within the threshold of it. So after each cluster is chosen, only seeds near the newly clustered points are recomputed, and their member lists are kept within a memory budget so the winner doesn't need rebuilding.\n4. **OpenMP.** The recomputed seeds are processed in parallel, largest first, with dynamic scheduling. The thread count is fixed after the first round, and rounds with little work run on a single thread. Picking the winning seed is parallelised for large N.\n\n**`CMakeLists.txt`:** adds `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` and links `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`. The executable name and compile flags are unchanged.\n\nThe original hangs in data generation for very small N (e.g. `-n 1`): it never creates any points when N is under 30.
